## Interpretation limits

- Co-occurrence and symbol references are static clues, not proof of runtime execution or call order.
- JADX output is partial; missing symbols or references are not proof that a feature is absent.
- Use this index to select exact candidates for a second, narrower call-chain review. Do not modify the APK based on this index alone.
